Stat Sniper compares lines across major sportsbooks and prediction markets in one view. The prop tracker highlights the best available price for each prop, and Chad factors line comparison into his picks, so you always know whether you&#x27;re getting the sharpest number before you bet.</p></div></details><details class="HomePage_faqItem__WIcco"><summary id="what-are-daily-edge-reports-summary" aria-controls="what-are-daily-edge-reports-content" class="HomePage_faqSummary__Z8myU"><span class="HomePage_faqNumber__hxTBy">12</span><span class="HomePage_faqQuestion__JBHfF">What are daily edge reports?</span><span class="HomePage_faqPlus__ewXUd" aria-hidden="true"></span></summary><div id="what-are-daily-edge-reports-content" role="region" aria-labelledby="what-are-daily-edge-reports-summary" class="HomePage_faqAnswer__iT5SB"><p>Every morning, Chad scans the full slate across all supported sports and ranks the day&#x27;s strongest picks before the lines move. Each one comes with a confidence rating, supporting data, and the reasoning behind the call, delivered free in the app before the action starts.</p></div></details><details class="HomePage_faqItem__WIcco"><summary id="is-stat-sniper-a-sportsbook-summary" aria-controls="is-stat-sniper-a-sportsbook-content" class="HomePage_faqSummary__Z8myU"><span class="HomePage_faqNumber__hxTBy">13</span><span class="HomePage_faqQuestion__JBHfF">Is Stat Sniper a sportsbook?</span><span class="HomePage_faqPlus__ewXUd" aria-hidden="true"></span></summary><div id="is-stat-sniper-a-sportsbook-content" role="region" aria-labelledby="is-stat-sniper-a-sportsbook-summary" class="HomePage_faqAnswer__iT5SB"><p>No. Stat Sniper never takes wagers or handles money on bets. It&#x27;s a free analysis and social app for bettors: Chad&#x27;s picks, the prop tracker, and the analytics dashboard help you make decisions, and any actual betting happens at the sportsbook of your choice.</p></div></details><details class="HomePage_faqItem__WIcco"><summary id="can-i-sell-my-picks-on-stat-sniper-summary" aria-controls="can-i-sell-my-picks-on-stat-sniper-content" class="HomePage_faqSummary__Z8myU"><span class="HomePage_faqNumber__hxTBy">14</span><span class="HomePage_faqQuestion__JBHfF">Can I sell my picks on Stat Sniper?</span><span class="HomePage_faqPlus__ewXUd" aria-hidden="true"></span></summary><div id="can-i-sell-my-picks-on-stat-sniper-content" role="region" aria-labelledby="can-i-sell-my-picks-on-stat-sniper-summary" class="HomePage_faqAnswer__iT5SB"><p>Yes. Stat Sniper&#x27;s picks marketplace lets you create subscription tiers, set your own pricing, and get paid via Stripe directly to your bank account. Your profile tracks your record, followers, and win rate, and you can earn a verified badge by building a proven track record. It&#x27;s built for anyone who wants to turn their betting edge into income.</p></div></details><details class="HomePage_faqItem__WIcco"><summary id="how-does-the-social-network-work-summary" aria-controls="how-does-the-social-network-work-content" class="HomePage_faqSummary__Z8myU"><span class="HomePage_faqNumber__hxTBy">15</span><span class="HomePage_faqQuestion__JBHfF">How does the social network work?</span><span class="HomePage_faqPlus__ewXUd" aria-hidden="true"></span></summary><div id="how-does-the-social-network-work-content" role="region" aria-labelledby="how-does-the-social-network-work-summary" class="HomePage_faqAnswer__iT5SB"><p>Stat Sniper is the only social network built specifically for sports bettors. It includes Personal, Following, and Discover feeds, live game-day chats with official rooms per game, private groups, DMs, and threaded comments. Share picks, debate matchups, follow sharp analysts, and build a reputation backed by your actual results.</p></div></details><details class="HomePage_faqItem__WIcco"><summary id="where-can-i-download-the-ai-sports-betting-app-summary" aria-controls="where-can-i-download-the-ai-sports-betting-app-content" class="HomePage_faqSummary__Z8myU"><span class="HomePage_faqNumber__hxTBy">16</span><span class="HomePage_faqQuestion__JBHfF">Where can I download the AI sports betting app?</span><span class="HomePage_faqPlus__ewXUd" aria-hidden="true"></span></summary><div id="where-can-i-download-the-ai-sports-betting-app-content" role="region" aria-labelledby="where-can-i-download-the-ai-sports-betting-app-summary" class="HomePage_faqAnswer__iT5SB"><p>Stat Sniper is available free on the App Store for iOS, Google Play for Android, and in any browser at app.statsniper.com. Download it and start getting Chad&#x27;s picks, tracking props, and connecting with other bettors, no subscription required.</p></div></details></div></div></div></section><section id="join-us" They look similar and they are not — they answer completely different questions, and reading them together is most of the skill.\",\"explainerEdgeTitle\":\"Edge %\",\"explainerEdgeBody\":\"Edge is how much better Chad thinks your odds are than the price you are being offered. Chad projects a win probability for the bet, converts the sportsbook's odds into their implied probability, strips out the vig so both numbers are on the same footing, and subtracts. What is left is the edge.\",\"explainerConfidenceTitle\":\"Confidence\",\"explainerConfidenceBody\":\"Confidence is how strongly Chad trusts its own projection — driven by data quality, sample size, and how stable the inputs are. It says nothing about the price. A pick can be high-confidence and still be a poor bet if the market has already priced it in.\",\"explainerExampleHeading\":\"A worked example\",\"explainerExampleSteps\":[\"Chad projects the Over to hit 58% of the time.\",\"The sportsbook is offering -130, which implies 56.5%.\",\"Strip the vig out of that line and the market's true number is 54%.\",\"58% − 54% = a 4% edge.\"],\"explainerExampleConclusion\":\"So a pick labelled \\\"12.0% Edge\\\" means Chad believes the real chance of that outcome is 12 percentage points higher than the market is pricing it. That is a large edge. As a rule of thumb Chad wants roughly 3%+ on game lines and 4%+ on player props before a bet earns a place on the board.\",\"explainerTogetherHeading\":\"Reading the two together\",\"explainerTogetherBody1\":\"Edge asks \\\"is this price wrong?\\\" Confidence asks \\\"how sure are we?\\\" They move independently. A heavy favorite can be a high-confidence projection with no edge at all, because the market already knows it is a heavy favorite. The bets worth your attention are the ones where a solid confidence rating meets a real edge.\",\"explainerTogetherBody2\":\"Edge is always measured against a specific price, so a worse number at your book shrinks it — and a bad enough number erases it. This guide explains how to read the spread, what it means to 'cover,' and how to find value — with AI-backed reads inside Stat Sniper.\",\"intro1\":\"A point spread is a margin the favorite must beat for your bet to win. The favorite is marked with a minus (e.g. −7.5) and must win by more than that number; the underdog is marked with a plus (e.g. +7.5) and can lose by fewer points — or win outright — and still cover.\",\"intro2\":\"Spreads exist to balance betting action on lopsided games. Instead of laying a huge moneyline price on a dominant favorite, you bet whether they win by enough. Most spreads are priced around −110 on each side, meaning you stake $110 to win $100.\",\"howHeading\":\"How the spread works\",\"how\":[{\"title\":\"Read the number\",\"body\":\"−7.5 means the favorite must win by 8 or more. +7.5 means the underdog covers by losing by 7 or fewer, or by winning the game.\"},{\"title\":\"Understand 'the hook'\",\"body\":\"The .5 is the hook. It removes ties (a 'push') by forcing the margin onto one side. A spread of −7 can push if the team wins by exactly 7; −7.5 cannot.\"},{\"title\":\"Mind the juice\",\"body\":\"Both sides are usually priced near −110. That margin is the book's cut, so winning at a break-even rate still loses money — you need roughly 52.4% to profit.\"}],\"exampleHeading\":\"Covering the spread in action\",\"exampleIntro\":\"How the final score decides a spread bet, win or lose.\",\"examples\":[{\"title\":\"Backing the favorite (−6.5)\",\"body\":\"You bet a −6.5 favorite.
